### Asar · A

LENS: Asar · A — OUTCOME TRUTH
GRADE: B — every mark, approve, hit, miss, lapse, manual reload and fire-refusal is one hashed row and the strip equals the record; but a TARGET press that finds nothing, a MARK refusal, a QUAL "one round per target" refusal and a full-magazine refusal leave no hashed trace, and two user paths zero the strip without a RESET row.
Deck verified: play.html == drone-2525_r.147.html, sha256 2476f68a… (sha256sum). Harnesses: drone-deck-qa 34/0 (boot QA 151/152 portrait+landscape) · asm-play solo lane 7 qual40 ×12 exit 0 · drone-team-e2e 9/0, host hash 55385386 == joiner. Probes: fleet147/asarA-probe.mjs (P1–P10) and asarA-probe2.mjs (P5b, P11–P15), headless Chromium 390×844, page errors 0.

HELD:
- T-box survives the head nudge (P4): pip 20 px beside the 150 R → targetN(1) marks C-150R-L01, aimUnitAt moves 0.63°, lockOn() then returns the marked ref via the hysteresis branch (play.html:1934-1937, dot>0.82), reticleNear() finds the pip on it, APPROVE reds, FIRE lands HIT CIRCLE. Gate: TARGET_MARKS_THE_PIP (:3996) — moved<1.5 && red && hit.
- Key 1 refuses beyond reach exactly where the button refuses (P1/P2): same lockOn()=null on the grass; targetN :2210 (n===1 && px>LOCK_REACH_PX) vs button :4393. Gate: TARGETN_HITS_THE_EXPOSED_PLATE (:3994) now aims 20 px off the plate — but NO gate asserts the refusal itself (see NOT HELD 1).
- One outcome = one hashed row on the record: DESIGNATED (:1918), APPROVE via decide (:2265→2222), HIT/MISS via netEvent (:2328), LAPSE (rangeLapse, :P9 two LAPSE rows for two unengaged windows, qualR 2, HUD "HIT 0/2 · NOT ENGAGED 2"), RELOAD (magLoad MANUAL → decide RELOAD, P8_reload_ok ev+1), every fireN refusal → decide REJECT/HOLD (:2346-2360). A hit adds SIM-ACTION + SCORE rows beside HIT (P4 rows) — three rows, one outcome, by design (decision, tally); not double-counted (metricsOf counts verbs, not points).
- Screen == record (P5b): 6 shots without a reset → 3 HIT rows / 3 MISS rows, strip "HIT 3 · MISS 3", hudScore :2309 reads state.rangeHit/rangeMiss which applyHit :2326 increments on the same branch that writes the row. replayHash stable and == metricsOf().replayHash (P5, P15: 0 uncommitted rows). Gate: HUD_SCORE_WRAPS + REDUCER_HIT_AND_LAPSE_DOWN_THE_PLATE.

NOT HELD (classes):
1. A refusal at MARK time is not a row, while the same refusal from a peer is. TARGET button on nothing: toast only (:4393) — P1: ev 0, replay 0, hash unmoved. Key 1 on nothing: log('TGT','none') to state.replay only (:2210) — P2: ev 0, replay +1; replayHash reads state.events (:2090), so the trace is outside the hash. Voice "target" on nothing: toast only (:3610). designate() refusals (round not open :1900, wrong lane :1902, target down :1903) toast only — P13: rows [] ; peerDesig writes HOLD/REJECT for the same cases (:2233-2234; P3b ev+1). Repro: aim at the sky, press TARGET, replayHash() unchanged. asm-play solo: 12 presses → 12 "NO TARGET UNDER THE BULLSEYE" toasts, 1 HIT on record, 0 refusal rows.
2. The TARGET button moves the head even when designate() refused. :4393 calls aimUnitAt after designate unconditionally; P3 (room WAITING): desig null, head moved 0.63°, no row. The optic obeys a mark the record says never happened.
3. Two refusals of a round are silent: QUAL second pull at an engaged plate → applyHit 'SPENT' returns REFUSED with no ev (:2319; P7 ev 0, hash unmoved) although QUAL_ONE_ROUND_PER_TARGET gates the band; RELOAD when full → toast only (:767; P8_reload_full ev 0) although the fifth magazine is a REJECT row (:768).
4. A QUAL shot at a standing plate outside the engagement is a MISS row that no tally sees: applyHit :2318 writes MISS 'MISS DOWN' and returns before qualRecordShot and before rangeMiss; fireN still magSpend()s (:2377, result 'MISS'≠'REFUSED'). P6: rows [MISS|C-50L-L01|MISS DOWN], qualR 0, roundSpent 1, HUD "HIT 0/0". Record says miss, magazine says fired, qualification says nothing.
5. The strip zeroes without a RESET row on two user paths: scene select modePick→goRange()→rangeReset() (:4639→:4625→:800) — P11: "HIT 3 · MISS 3" → "HIT 0 · MISS 0", rows []; lanePick→seatLane→goRange (:4634) same. The RESET button and the mode select DO write RESET|RANGE (P12, P14 via rangeResetOnRecord :4556). Replay of the record shows 6 shots; the screen showed 0.

OWED r.148:
1. One refusal primitive for MARK: designate()/targetN()/button/voice write decide('REJECT'|'HOLD', id|'NONE', {reason:'NO_LOCK'|'ROUND_NOT_OPEN'|'WRONG_LANE'|'TARGET_DOWN', via:'BUTTON'|'KEY'|'VOICE'}) exactly as peerDesig and fireN do; button aims only when designate returned a mark. Gate: MARK_REFUSAL_IS_A_ROW (sky → replayHash moves by one REJECT row; key 1, button and voice produce the same verb/reason) + TARGET_REFUSED_HEAD_STAYS.
2. Every silent refusal on the shot path is a row: SPENT → decide('REJECT',id,{reason:'ONE_ROUND_PER_TARGET'}); MAG FULL → decide('REJECT','MAG',{reason:'MAGAZINE_FULL'}); MISS DOWN in QUAL either refuses (no round spent, REJECT 'NOT_THE_ENGAGEMENT') or counts (qualRecordShot) — never a MISS row with no tally. Gate: extend QUAL_ONE_ROUND_PER_TARGET and RELOAD_ON_RECORD to assert ev+1; new QUAL_OUT_OF_ENGAGEMENT_IS_ONE_THING.
3. rangeReset() is never reached from a user control without rangeResetOnRecord(): goRange() takes a why and writes RESET|RANGE (scene/lane change), or the strip is not zeroed. Gate: STRIP_ZERO_HAS_A_RESET_ROW (change scene with HIT 3 · MISS 3 on the strip → exactly one RESET row, HUD 0/0).

HARDWARE-GATED: a phone's real speech recognizer on "target one/two" (the closure :3608 marks lockOn() as slot N — "target two" marks the target under the pip, not T2 — only a live mic proves what a human hears back); two real phones' clocks for LAPSE rows (host clock, lapseIsMine); touch on the 11 px 150 R plate at 390 px width; a human reading the strip while the RESET-less zero happens.

### Asar · B

LENS: Asar · B — OUTCOME TRUTH (adversarial play) · deck r.147 sha256 2476f68a… · probes: fleet147/asar-b-probe.mjs (39 steps, solo), asar-b-ai-probe.mjs, asar-b-team-probe.mjs (19 steps, two contexts over WebRTC); harness: asar-b-{qual,stay,dbl,ai,team}.json
GRADE: C+ — every round that leaves the barrel is on the record and both phones carry the same hash, but a FIRE refusal names a target nobody marked, and r.147's own headline refusal (nothing under the bullseye) leaves no row at all.

BROKE
1. THE REJECT ROW NAMES A TARGET THE SHOOTER NEVER MARKED. fireN (play.html:2347) takes `s = state.desig || slots().find(n)` — slots() is T1 nearest the pip with NO reach limit — so a FIRE with no box is booked against whatever is nearest on the picture.
   solo step 04: pip on grass, TARGET just refused ("NO TARGET UNDER THE BULLSEYE") → screen "NO RED BOX · MARK A TARGET FIRST", record `REJECT|C-150R-L08|NO_RED_BOX` (a plate 200 px off, refused by r.147's own rule).
   solo step 09 / team steps 09–10: FIRE again after the 50 R fell → screen "NO RED BOX · MARK A TARGET FIRST", record `REJECT|C-100R-L08|NO_RED_BOX` on BOTH phones; the other seat reads "THE OTHER SEAT · REJECT C-100R-L08 · NO RED BOX". The lie travels and is hashed.
2. QUAL "ONE ROUND PER TARGET" (SPENT) IS OFF THE RECORD. applyHit (:2320) returns REFUSED with a toast only; fireN writes `FIRE` to state.replay, never to events.
   solo steps 24, 25, 27: three "ONE ROUND PER TARGET" on screen → Δevents 0, Δdecisions 0, hash unmoved (297a… stays). Worse, step 26–27: key 1 re-marks the already-engaged 50 R (DESIGNATED row), APPROVE reds it (APPROVE row), FIRE is refused (no row) — the record reads DESIGNATED → APPROVE → nothing, an engagement left open that the screen closed.
3. THE MARK'S AND THE APPROVAL'S REFUSALS ARE NOT ROWS; THE TRIGGER'S ARE. TARGET button on grass (solo 01, team 01): 0 rows anywhere (fTgt :4393 toasts and returns); key 1 beyond reach (solo 02, team 02): replay-only `TGT|none|1` (targetN :2210 → log()); APPROVE with no amber (solo 03): nothing (:2268); ALL DOWN (solo 37–38): nothing; RELOAD when full (solo 15, team 18): nothing. Hash identical to a session where none of it happened. In the fleet harness these were 11/12 (qual40), 10/11 (stay), 3/4 (bounce), 2/3 (team) of the engagements — every one counted "refusal" on screen, none in the hash. r.134/r.135 made RESET and a refused wire approval rows; r.147's own refusal is the one with no row.
4. AI SEAT: THE HIT IS A ROW THE SCREEN NEVER SHOWS. After FIRE the "HIT · IN THE AIMING CIRCLE" toast lives 43–76 ms before asmTick's next "AMBER · … MARKED BY THE AI" overwrites it (6/6 shots; 3/6 already gone at the first 49 ms read). Record: 6 HIT rows, strip HIT 6 · MISS 0. The fleet harness read hits 2 · misses 2 from that screen (asar-b-ai.json vs final rangeHit 4). r.133 promised "a long sentence stays up long enough to be read".
HELD
- FIRE on amber → REJECT AMBER_NO_APPROVE; empty magazine → REJECT EMPTY_MAGAZINE (no round spent: mag 1/0 → RELOAD row MANUAL → 2/30); after a lapse → REJECT NONE|NO_RED_BOX (screen "YOUR TARGET LAPSED"); RELOAD → RELOAD|MANUAL. Each one row, one decision, hash moves.
- HIT/MISS/LAPSE reconcile: 42 HIT rows = state.hits 42 = 30 + 1 + 11 across three tables; MISS row ↔ qual 0/1; LAPSE row ↔ "NOT ENGAGED · 100 M CENTRE · UNFIRED MISS · 0/2" ↔ rangeLapsed 1 ↔ qualExpired 1 ↔ qualR 2; metricsOf().authFailures 5 = REJECT rows 5; pack().events/replayHash = live (227 / 90ad3977).
- RESET double-ask: first press "PRESS RESET AGAIN TO DISCARD 2 ROUNDS" = no row (an ask, not an outcome); second = `RESET|RANGE|BUTTON qualR 2 qualH 0`, strip HIT 0/0; a third press on an empty table is another RESET row (harmless, honest).
- SAVE ("SAVED 860") and EXPORT ("WIRE") do not move the hash (replay-only SAVE row). Plate return in RESET mode: no row, derivable from the RESET row's mode + RETURN_S.
- Two phones: TWO_HUMANS HOLD, AMBER_NO_APPROVE, NOT_THE_MARKERS_SEAT, NO_RED_BOX, MISS×2 (training: red holds after a miss), RESET (+AUTHORITY_RELEASED on each side) — every row on both phones, hash equal at all 19 steps (21ee5618).
- Fleet instrument note: asm-play.mjs classifies by the toast it reads immediately after the click; on the AI seat that toast is already the next mark. Its hits/misses are not evidence; the strip and the rows are.
OWED r.148
1. A FIRE with no red box is booked against NONE (or the last marked id), never slots()[T1] — `fireN` :2347 — gate `REJECT_NEVER_NAMES_AN_UNMARKED_TARGET` (deck QA + range-2525: pip on grass, FIRE → row.id==='NONE').
2. SPENT is a row: `decide('REJECT',id,{reason:'ONE_ROUND_PER_TARGET'})` in applyHit :2320, and designate()/targetN refuse an `_eng` plate in QUAL with a HOLD `TARGET_SPENT` — gate `SPENT_IS_A_ROW` (MISS → FIRE → Δevents 1, hash moves; key 1 on the engaged plate → no DESIGNATED row).
3. The mark's and the approval's refusals are HOLD rows (`NO_TARGET_UNDER_PIP`, `NO_AMBER`, `ALL_DOWN`, `MAGAZINE_FULL`) for the TARGET button, key 1, APPROVE and RELOAD alike, and the AI seat's next mark waits for the HIT sentence's dwell — gate `REFUSAL_IS_A_ROW` (every toast that begins NO/ALL DOWN/MAGAZINE FULL ⇒ Δevents=1) + `HIT_TOAST_READABLE` (≥1400 ms before asmTick re-marks).
HARDWARE-GATED: real thumb on the HEAD stick (the probes set pan/tilt; only the buttons and keys were pressed), two physical phones over real WebRTC, the laser-tag receiver's hit vs pipOff<32 px, and toast dwell on a phone GPU under load. Nothing above needs hardware to reproduce.

### Athena · A

LENS: Athena · A — THE STRANGER
GRADE: C — with no mark outstanding r.147 marks what the bullseye is on every time (408/408, 120/120 grass, 280/280 chains, both orientations, 1×/3×, RESET/DOWN/QUAL·40); the moment the stranger holds an unfinished amber/red, TARGET ignores the bullseye and swings back to the old mark (36/36) — the operator's "keeps resetting" sentence is still alive — and the portrait top lines overlap on every mark.

THE 60 SECONDS (play.html == r.147, byte-identical; probes athena-a-*.mjs + captures in fleet147/):
1. 4 taps: CONTINUE/SKIP (line 181) → SELECT CITY · NEXT (192) → SELECT CRAFT, QUAD lit (198) · NEXT → SELECT RANGE, CH0 TRAIN lit · PRACTICE · NO ROOM (213). He lands in a TURRET on lane 21 whatever craft was lit (goRange 4608) with the bullseye ALREADY on the 50 R (rangeFirstPlate 803 → aimUnitAt 4626). Capture stranger-portrait-01-start.png: strip "CH0 D3 TARGET FIRST", LOCK C-50-L21 50M, toast "CH0 PRACTICE · NO ROOM".
2. TARGET, stick untouched → "AMBER · 50 M · NOW PRESS APPROVE", strip "AMBER T1 C-50-L21" (designate 1919, hudPhase 2714). APPROVE → "RED BOX · 50 M · APPROVED BY YOU · NOW PRESS FIRE" (2287), strip "RED T1 C-50-L21". FIRE → "HIT · IN THE AIMING CIRCLE", strip "LAST T1 C-50-L21 DOWN" (2715). ≈10 s, both orientations (athena-a-stranger.json).
3. Stick to the next plate: a 120 ms tap at ¼ deflection moves 25 px at 1× and 3× (r.141 zf, 2656), reach is 48 px (1928), a 150 m plate is 11 px wide at 1× — landable, fiddly; a full-deflection tap is 154 px, three plates past (athena-a-stick.mjs). Bullseye on grass or sky: "NO TARGET UNDER THE BULLSEYE · PUT IT ON ONE" (1927; 120/120).
4. WHERE IT FAILS: he marked the 50 but did not finish it (or fired and missed — a miss keeps the red, 2380), puts the bullseye dead-centre on the 150 R, presses TARGET → lockOn 1934-1937 returns the OLD mark inside a 35° cone (dot>0.82), designate re-ambers it, 4393 swings the head 2.27° back: "AMBER · 100 M CENTRE" while he is looking at the 150. 36/36: amber/red/missed-red × RESET/DOWN/QUAL·40 × 1×/3× × portrait/landscape (athena-a-hyst.json a,b,c). No pill on the face clears a mark; only a kill does (2379). FIRE with red on A while looking at B → "MISS" scored on A, a QUAL round spent (12/12, scenario f).

HELD / NOT HELD:
HELD H1 pip rule with no mark outstanding — 408/408 marks = the plate under/nearest the bullseye, 0 grass marks, 280/280 amber→red→HIT on the marked id, head swing on TARGET ≤1.38° (median 0.31°), qual40 56/56 (athena-a-matrix.json; pipRank/pipNearest 1929-1930, r.143 pass 1940). Kill → next TARGET takes the pip (16/16, scenarios d/e).
HELD H2 the words — grass/sky "NO TARGET UNDER THE BULLSEYE · PUT IT ON ONE"; DOWN with every plate down "ALL DOWN · RESET"; QUAL gap "NO TARGET UP · NEXT IN 2.5 S" / "7.0 S"; "QUAL COMPLETE · RESET FOR ANOTHER" (1927; athena-a-msgs.mjs).
HELD H3 landscape top lines disjoint (#board y112 vs #playHud 74-100); FULL hides both (line 29). Deck QA: drone-deck-qa 34/0, boot 151/152 red=SYNC_DIRECT.
NOT HELD N1 mark hysteresis (class, step 4): lockOn 1934-1937; REVISIONS.md:521 keeps it as a feature ("the marked target's hysteresis … stay"). Repro: aim C-100C, TARGET, aim C-150R, TARGET → marked C-100C-L21, head -2.27°.
NOT HELD N2 portrait top-line overlap is a CLASS, not the 1× canvas: #playHud top:42px (line 28, r.138) sits on #board top:44px (line 37, list() 2415) — "CH0 D3 AMBER T1 C-150R-L21" @y224 under "T1 AMBER C-150R-L21" @y226 at 320/390/430 wide, 1× AND 3×, whenever any slot is amber or red (athena-a-overlap.mjs; capture overlap-portrait-1x-amber-150R.png — the 2× "y≈460" is CSS 230). TARGET follows the bullseye with a mark outstanding: keep the hysteresis only while the pip is within LOCK_REACH_PX of the marked target (1934-1937 → reticleNear 1923), else mark the plate under the pip and release the old amber/unfired red on the record. ### Athena · B

LENS: Athena · B — THE STRANGER (adversarial play)
Deck: frontend/public/drone-2525/play.html sha256 2476f68a… (r.147). Headless Chromium 1194, fresh context each run, 390×844 then 844×390, DPR 2, CDP touch events only (no keys, no state writes). Probes + captures: scratchpad/fleet147/athenaB/ (probe.mjs · probe4.mjs · probe5.mjs · athenaB*.json · *.png).

GRADE: D — the operator's sentence "target approve and fire keeps resetting to 50 m left target" reproduces verbatim on r.147 in both orientations: once a mark exists, TARGET ignores the bullseye, re-marks the 50 L and swings the head 7.4–7.6° back onto it (p5-*-04, p4-*-hyst-*).

THE 60 SECONDS (portrait, turret, timings from first paint; landscape identical unless noted)
 0.4 s  intro paints ("A wireframe point. Then the Capitol."). CONTINUE ×11 to reach SELECT CITY (3.6 s); landscape SKIP TO SELECT (0.8 s).
 5.3 s  ENTER WAITING ROOM → a 1,255 px-tall page; START · HOST STARTS → toast "ROOM HOLD · HOST STARTS" over the footer text (portrait-05). Alone, dead end; nothing says "press HOST ROOM". BACK TO SETUP → PRACTICE.
 6.3 s  table. The deck parks the head ON the 50 L (pan −5.26, pip 0 px from C-50-L21, portrait-06). Strip reads "TARGET FIRST".
 6.6 s  TARGET, stick untouched → "AMBER · 50 M · NOW PRESS APPROVE" (portrait-07). FIRE now → "AMBER · PRESS APPROVE FIRST". APPROVE → "RED BOX · 50 M · APPROVED BY YOU · NOW PRESS FIRE". FIRE → "HIT · IN THE AIMING CIRCLE". FIRST HIT 7.3 s portrait / 4.4 s landscape — on the plate the deck chose, not one I chose.
 8–50 s the stick. Full deflection = 37°/s portrait, 18–20°/s landscape (frame-bound); a 100 ms thumb flick = 5.6° / 2.5° — the whole far cluster (50 L → 300 spans ~3.2° of pan, plates 10–40 px apart at 1×). No pan stop: 12 s of stick = −95°, the range gone, toast "NO TARGET UNDER THE BULLSEYE · PUT IT ON ONE" with nothing on the picture and no hint which way. Look-nudge-look reaches a named plate in 0.3–1.8 s (4–9 nudges).
 50–58 s with NO mark live, TARGET is honest: 10/11 plates — pip on it → marked it, head moved ≤0.4°, APPROVE reds, FIRE hits (p4-*-T-*.png, e.g. "AMBER · 150 M RIGHT · NOW PRESS APPROVE"). The 11th: pip 13 px on the 300, the 200 R sits 10 px away → marks the 200 R; at 1× a phone cannot separate them.
 THEN the complaint (p5, both orientations): TARGET as parked → 50 L amber. Stick → pip 4 px on the 150 R. TARGET → "AMBER · 50 M · NOW PRESS APPROVE", head snaps to pan −5.26 (Δ 3.2° here, 7.5° from the 100 R). Stick back to the 150 R. APPROVE → "RED BOX · 50 M …" (red box on the 50 L, ring on the 150 R). FIRE → "MISS", MAG 29. TARGET → 50 L again + snap. Six APPROVE+FIRE from the 150 R = six "MISS", six rounds, red box never leaves the 50 L (p5-portrait-08: ring on the far cluster, red box lower right "T1 · 50M F · RED", dock "LOCK C-50-L21 50M", strip "HIT 0 · MISS 1"). Only exit found by a finger: tap the plate on the picture → "AMBER · 150 M RIGHT", strip "AMBER T2 C-150R-L21" — a second slot, the 50 L still marked.
 double-tap TARGET/FIRE: one act, one toast. Hold FIRE 1.5 s: one refusal, no auto-fire. RELOAD full → "MAGAZINE FULL · 30 RDS". APPROVE with nothing → "NO AMBER · MARK A TARGET FIRST". RESET → "RANGE RESET · ON THE RECORD", mark cleared, head still on the 50 L. Rotate mid-round: state kept, layout reflows, nothing lost. FULL: TARGET·APPROVE·FIRE·RELOAD·EXIT all top-most and working (portrait-23). MAP/OPERATOR: back with state intact.

BROKE (repro + capture)
 1. THE MARK OUTRANKS THE EYE. lockOn() line 1935–1937 returns state.desig.ref whenever it is inside a 35° cone (dot>0.82), before the r.147 pip rule runs; TARGET then aimUnitAt()s the head onto it (line 4393). Repro: PRACTICE → TARGET → stick to any other plate → TARGET. p5-portrait-04-target-at-150R.png, p4-*-hyst-second-target.png. Same class: APPROVE reds what the seat is no longer looking at; FIRE scores "MISS" against a box 100+ px from the ring (p5-*-08). The r.147 gates (LOCK_IS_NEAREST_TO_THE_PIP, TARGET_MARKS_THE_PIP) run with desig=null — the stranger never has desig=null after his first press ("TARGET FIRST").
 2. THE STRIP COLLIDES WITH THE CANVAS LINE AT 390. DOM #playHud "CH0 D3 AMBER T1 C-50-L21" at CSS y 224–250 and the canvas "T1 AMBER C-50-L21" (hud fillText) drawn on the same line → unreadable double print, portrait-07 / portrait-31 / p5-portrait-08 at y≈460 (2×). Landscape draws the canvas line one row lower (landscape-31), no collision. Also the left-clamped captions pile: "150M E" over "100M F" at x=6 (portrait-06).
 3. "MISS" IS THE WRONG WORD. The seat looked at the 150 R, the deck fired at the 50 L, and told him he missed. Six rounds gone; no sentence says "your red box is on the 50 M, your ring is not".
 4. Minor: START · HOST STARTS alone → "ROOM HOLD · HOST STARTS" and no way forward named; landscape bar row 2 overflows to x=1190 (RESET·CH·DIFF·HAL off the right edge until the bar is swiped); MAP legend "MAP · 42 LANES · tap L## · QUAL gold · UP green · DOWN bl…" cut at the right edge under the R HEAD stick (portrait-29); no pan stop / no "range is that way" when the picture is empty.
HELD: every refusal spoke (none silent); the amber/red/hit words; FULL keeps the four controls; rotation; RELOAD full; hold/double-tap; the fresh-mark rule on 10/11 plates in both orientations; a finger on the plate marks it.

OWED r.148 (ordered)
 1. Invariant: "TARGET marks what the bullseye is on, whether or not something is already marked." Drop the desig-first return in lockOn() when the pip has a candidate within LOCK_REACH_PX; keep the old mark only when the pip is on nothing. Gate: TARGET_FOLLOWS_THE_EYE — mark 50 L, put the pip on the 150 R, TARGET → 150 R, head moves <0.5°; plus FIRE_SAYS_WHERE — with a red box off the ring, FIRE reads "YOUR RED BOX IS ON THE 50 M · YOUR RING IS NOT · PRESS TARGET", and never spends the round.
 2. One HUD line at 390: the canvas "T1 AMBER …" line yields to #playHud (or draws below it). Gate: STRIP_NEVER_UNDER_CANVAS_TEXT — plateCaptionRects/hud text rects vs #playHud/#magBar rects, portrait and landscape, zero intersection; the left-clamped captions stack without overlap.
 3. The picture is never empty without a pointer: a pan stop at the range edges or an arrow "RANGE ←" when no plate projects; a stick rate that is time-based, not frame-based (37°/s vs 19°/s between orientations). Gate: STICK_RATE_IS_SECONDS + EMPTY_PICTURE_POINTS_HOME.

HARDWARE-GATED: real thumb on glass (touch jitter, 60 vs 120 Hz stick rate), a phone's actual FPS for the stick's °/s, the waiting-room path with a second device (HOST/JOIN), VOICE.

### Christo · A

LENS: Christo · A — THE TWO-PHONE ROOM (r.147, play.html == drone-2525_r.147.html, cmp identical)
GRADE: B — the mark travels as the id and the approver is turned onto the right plate at any zoom/orientation, one hash on both phones; but the approver's own TARGET press silently re-authors the host's mark against its own pip, and a refused peer mark is announced as AMBER.

HELD (probes: fleet147/christo-probe.log · christo-probe2.log · christo-e2e.log; two isolated Chromium contexts over WebRTC, host 390×844 @1×, joiner 844×390 @3×)
- The MARK is the id, never the pixel. designate() sends {id,kind,slot,phase,event} (play.html:1920); peerDesig resolves the id in the receiver's own pool (2231-2232); approveDesig/fireN act on state.desig.id (2265-2290, 2347). Probe S1: host pip 20 px beside the 150 R → host marks C-150R-L21; joiner (pan 70°, 3×, landscape) holds the same id, amber, by the host. S2 mirror (joiner marks C-300 at 3×, host at 1×/pan −60) held too.
- "Look at what you approve" holds under a different picture: peerDesig aimUnitAt (2240) is world-space (1104-1106); joiner pipOff 0 px and pip inside the marked plate's outline at 3× landscape (S1), host likewise at 1× (S2). Zoom only scales focalPx (786); the aim is angular.
- A peer DESIG for a target off the approver's picture is sane: pipRank's off-picture rule (1929) governs only the LOCAL lock; the wire path never checks the picture and swings the head onto it (S1: joiner started 70° away). Other lane → HOLD PEER_MARK_OTHER_LANE row (2233), host told "THE OTHER SEAT · HOLD … PEER MARK OTHER LANE" (S3/S4).
- One canonical record: host commits, joiner's rows go SEQ_SUBMIT→SEQ_COMMIT (1192-1215, 1445-1462); replayHash over committed rows only (2089-2103). S1, S2, S4: identical hash + MATCH on both after mark/approve/fire and after a cross-lane HOLD. Stock gate `node scripts/drone-team-e2e.mjs`: 9/9, hash 6ffa9c48 == 6ffa9c48.

NOT HELD
1. THE APPROVER'S TARGET STEALS THE MARK (class: hysteresis before the pip rule + designate() re-authors). lockOn returns state.desig.ref whenever it is inside a 35° cone (1935-1937) BEFORE the r.147 pip rule; designate() then overwrites tgtSlot/desig with by:SID (1913-1914) and #fTgt swings the head back onto it (4393). Probe S5: host amber on 150 R; joiner puts its pip INSIDE the 50 L and presses TARGET → joiner lockOn = C-150R-L21, joiner desig by=joiner how LOCAL, host desig by=joiner how PEER, joiner head snapped from the 50 L back to the 150 R. Roles silently flip (host now approves, joiner fires) and the marker's own pip was never honoured. Repro: probe2 S5.
2. A REFUSED PEER MARK IS ANNOUNCED AS AMBER. commIn DESIG canonical branch builds msg:'NET AMBER '+id regardless of r.accepted (1513-1515); the reducer had already HELD it (2233). Probe S3/S4: joiner on another lane, no box, toast "NET AMBER C-100C-L21". Also a raw id, not plateWord — signer lexicon.
3. TRANSIENT FALSE DIVERGED ON THE HOST (race). The joiner's ACK hash is computed while its own HOLD row is still pending (excluded, 2090) but the host has already committed that row from the HOLD envelope (1470-1474) → host sync=DIVERGED with EQUAL hashes (probe1 S3: f94c5c0b/f94c5c0b, DIVERGED); heals on the 4 s HELLO (1597); probe2 S4 was MATCH at 1.2 s (race-dependent).
4. Code-read, not probed: peerDesig aims only a turret (2240 `u.kind==='turret'`) — a quad-seat approver is not turned onto a peer's mark.

OWED r.148 (ordered)
1. designate()/lockOn: when the pip sits inside ANOTHER plate's outline, the pip rule wins over the marked target's hysteresis (r.143 plate-under-pip pass before 1935); and a seated peer's amber with the same id is never re-authored by a local TARGET (keep by/how, or refuse "THE OTHER SEAT MARKED IT · APPROVE OR MARK ANOTHER"). Gate: two-context row TARGET_OVER_PEER_AMBER_KEEPS_AUTHOR in drone-team-e2e.mjs + range-2525 unit row PIP_IN_OTHER_PLATE_BEATS_HYSTERESIS.
2. commIn DESIG canonical branch: msg from the reducer's verdict (HOLD reason → sentence, plateWord, no id). Gate: QA row PEER_MARK_REFUSED_SAYS_HOLD + sign-lexicon-voice test extended to deck toasts (no /C-\d+/ ids).
3. ACK after commit: ACK/HELLO hash must include the acker's pending rows or be sent after SEQ_COMMIT lands. Gate: drone-team-e2e reads host state.sync ≤ 300 ms after a joiner HOLD and asserts MATCH (10 runs).

(3) A puts the bullseye 1 finger-width beside the 150 R, presses TARGET: expect amber 150 R on A; B's head swings onto the 150 R at 3×, amber box on the plate, "MARKED BY …". (4) A presses APPROVE → "TWO HUMANS". (5) B presses APPROVE → red on both. (6) A FIRE → HIT on A, "THE OTHER SEAT HIT · 150 M RIGHT" on B, MORE→hash equal, MATCH both. ### Odin · A

LENS: Odin · A — FUTURE-PROOF
GRADE: B− — the rule itself is angular-invariant and costs nothing at any scale in sight, but it is one rule in one function: the AI member, the T1·T2·T3 pool, the hysteresis and the record each still carry a different rule, and the seams open exactly where the game grows (rooms with an AI seat, aircraft, movers, two phones).
(play.html == drone-2525_r.147.html, cmp identical; all measurements headless Chromium 1194, served over node:http)

HOLDS AT SCALE
- Q1 angle: LOCK_REACH_PX=48 (L1928) divided by focalPx() (L786, EYE L785) is 26.26 mrad at 1× on 428×926 DPR1, 428×926 DPR3, 926×428 DPR3, 1920×1080 DPR1 and DPR2 — identical, because layout() sets the canvas backing to CSS px (L1140, no DPR scale anywhere: grep devicePixelRatio = 0 hits) and proj() uses focal not H (L1128). A 2× phone does NOT get half the reach. Zoom: 13.1 mrad @2×, 6.6 @4×, 0.88 mrad @30× (26 cm at 300 m; 11 cm at a 120 m UAV) — declared "finer as the optic zooms", true, and at 30× it is finer than a hand.
- Q2 cost: 2.15 lockOn()/draw (draw L1791 + reticleNear→lockOn L1924) + one per sample() tick (L2139); 7.3 platesHere()/draw, each a 462-plate filter (L968); 748 proj()/draw (all wire). pipRank on 617 candidates (462 plates + 129 boards + 8 pops + 12 UAV + 6 foils): 0.179 ms; lockOn range 0.046 ms; Capital all-up 0.085 ms. CPU throttled 8× (Pi-class emulation): 40 fps range and Capital, segs 280/cap, dropped 436 (range) / 1924 (Capital) — the segment budget bites, the lock never does (<1% of HAL.PI 66 ms, L646). Linear: the lock would need ~50,000 candidates on one picture to cost 15 ms. 21v21 / Austin do not reach it.
- Q4 wire: DESIG rows carry id·kind·slot only (L1921) — the px never crosses the wire, so screen size cannot fork the ledger.

BREAKS AT SCALE
- Q3 two authorities, and the AI wins by moving the human's head. Pip 19 px beside the 150 R, ASM SPOT on: asmTick (L2495) picks nearest IN METRES from the pit (L2504-2505) = C-50L (50 m vs 150 m), designates it as ASM@ (L2510), then aimUnitAt(su,best) (L2511) swings the human's turret 6.77° onto the 50 L; the human's lockOn now returns C-50L by hysteresis, slots T1 = C-50L@0 px, TARGET marks C-50L. That is the operator's r.147 symptom ("keeps resetting to 50 m left") re-entering through the AI door — in any room with an AI seat (3v3, 9v9, MASS-AI on the ground) the pip rule is overruled the moment ASM SPOT is on. With hiLock on the swing is 0°. REVISIONS r.147 "still open" names asmTick but not that it moves the head.
- Q5 movers: two UAVs crossing the pip at 8.2 m/s (CH2 spawn speed, L2542) at 60 m: in reach 11 of 60 frames, T1 flips 4× while unmarked; at 4× zoom in reach 3 frames, 2 flips. Once marked, hysteresis is the r.131 35° cone (L1936 dot>0.82, no reach term) — LOCK followed the UAV to 737 px, the picture's edge, for 90 frames. So "nearest the bullseye" is true only before the first mark; after it the rule is the old cone.
- slots() (T1·T2·T3, keys 1-3, voice) is a second pool (L2198): plates + pops only — no UAV, foil, ring, buoy, door — so from any turret at CH2+ keys 1-3 can never name an aircraft while TARGET can; and at chNum()!==0 it ranks the undrawn QUAL template (drawPlates L1869 draws PLATES): T01 at CH2 listed T1 = C-300@390 px, a plate that is not on the picture. targetN's reach check is n===1 only (L2210): key 2/3 mark beyond reach.
- Q4 clock/record: no canonical range clock (REVISIONS.md L297, L319; r.147 L530 "everything r.146 owed"); rangeTick is per device (L817), so the candidate pool (which plates are up) differs per phone. The lock is now a function of (cam, zoom, W, H, up/down) and sample() records lock id but neither zoom nor W·H (L2139-2147): an audit cannot re-derive a lock from the row, and a peer replaying the shooter's aim on another screen gets a different pool ("off the picture is never a candidate", L1929). Net: MATCH is not harder on the wire, but harder to prove, until the EXPOSE row lands. plan L2017: orderKey unsorted before 3v3 still stands.

OWED r.148
1. ONE POOL — slots() reads lockOn's candidate list (lift `cand` building out of lockOn; slots = pipRank(cand).slice(0,3)) and targetN checks reach for every n. Gate: range-2525 `SLOTS_ARE_THE_LOCK_POOL` (T1..T3 ⊆ lockOn candidates; at CH2 a UAV under the pip is T1; no QUAL template from a Capital seat; key 2 beyond reach refuses).
2. THE AI SPOTS THROUGH THE PIP, NEVER THE HAND — asmTick ranks by pipRank from the seat's camera (or by its own mount's pipRank), and L2511 never moves a seated human's head unless the human asked (hiLock semantics inverted to opt-in). Gate: `ASM_NEVER_MOVES_THE_PIP` (pip 20 px beside the 150 R, ASM SPOT on → head 0.00°, mark = 150 R or none) + the 3v3 lobby row.
3. THE MARK HOLDS ON THE PICTURE AND THE ROW REPLAYS IT — hysteresis bounded to on-picture within k·LOCK_REACH_PX (mover walks out → LOCK drops, record says so); sample() carries zoom·W·H; the r.137 EXPOSE row from the host so every device ranks the same standing plates. Gate: `LOCK_REPLAYS_FROM_THE_ROW` (re-derive lock from row fields = recorded lock, 100/100 rows) + two-phone MATCH.

HARDWARE-GATED
- 30× on a real DPR-3 phone: 0.88 mrad reach vs hand tremor — headless cannot hold a phone.
- Pi-class fps: emulation says 40 fps at 8× CPU throttle with rendering, not the lock, as the cost; a real Pi GPU and a real 1080p stream are not emulated.
- Two-phone MATCH with the lock rule (per-device clock) — needs two devices on one room.

### Odin · B

LENS: Odin · B — FUTURE-PROOF (measured)
GRADE: C — r.147's pipRank is cheap (≤35 µs, ≤7 % of a frame) and the reach is DPR-invariant, but the frame already falls under the Pi floor at 20× throttle, the fps instrument cannot read below 20 so the HAL ladder never sees it, and at 30× a Capital door 1,774 px tall is lost 1 mrad off its centre.
METHOD: playwright chromium-1194, 390×844 (canvas 390×662 CSS), node:http over frontend/public, CDP setCPUThrottlingRate 1/6/20, 5 s per cell; "rAF" = state.tickN/wall-clock (true), "state.fps" = the deck's own reading. Harness odinB-measure.mjs · odinB-grid.json · odinB-dpr.json · odinB-scaled.json/log (scaled copy sha fa2f6801…, repo untouched, sha 2476f68a…).

| range 462 plates, lane 20 (T21), pip 20 px beside 150 R | 1.1 free: 1× 57.1(56) · 6× 29.2(42) · 20× 10.0(24) | 1.1 marked: 55.5 · 45.3 · 19.4(25) | lockOn 35 µs free / 1 µs marked · draw 0.5–1.2 ms |
| range | 5.5 free: 60.1 · 30.1(33) · 14.2(27) | 5.5 marked: 38.7 · 36.4 · 18.5(35) | lockOn 34 / 0.5 µs · draw 0.8–2.5 ms (1,157 segs) |
| Capital T01, door D-11TH @68 m | 1.1 free: 57.7 · 39.3 · 15.8(23) | 1.1 marked: 59.9 · 40.1 · 16.5 | lockOn 23.5 / 0.5 µs · draw 0.4–1.0 ms |
| Capital T01 | 5.5 free: 60 · 39.3 · 18.1(26) | 5.5 marked: 59.2 · 37.1 · 18.3 | lockOn 27.5 / 0.5 µs |

| Capital D1Q | 5.5 free: 59.1 · 43.9 · 16.9(26) | 5.5 marked: 59.7 · 44.3 · 19.7 | lockOn 16.5 / 0.5 µs · draw 0.7–1.0 ms (1,443 segs) |
| SCALED COPY 1,386 plates (33/lane), 42 lanes + 42 turrets drawn | 1.1 free: 1× 59.5 · 6× 30.0(28.5) · 20× 10.8(21.6) | 1.1 marked: 59.8 · 36.9 · 13.8 | lockOn 97.5 / 1 µs · draw 3.0–4.5 ms · 35,535 segs dropped/draw | 5.5 free: 1× 59.6 · 6× 32.1(35.4) · 20× 13.2(24.2) | 5.5 marked: 60.1 · 36.5 · 14.4 | lockOn 65 / 0.5 µs · draw 4.0–5.1 ms |
CALLS PER draw() (wrapped globals, 2 s at 1×): lockOn 2.17 (draw + reticleNear→lockOn + sample every 1/hz) · pipRank 2.17 range / 4.35 Capital (pool + doors pool) · 0 when marked (hysteresis returns before the pool) · platesHere 7.3 range free (462-plate filter ×7 = 3,234 iterations/draw), 3 marked · plateRect 46.8 range free / 23 marked · proj 751 (range 1.1) → 3,275 (range 5.5) → 3,543–4,698 (Capital; 2,000+ segs projected then culled behind the camera). lockOn's share of proj ≈ 88/751 on the range (12 %), ≈ 3 % on the Capital. At MoT 5.5 draw runs at 19 Hz (spec) while rAF holds 60 → lockOn/rAF 0.54.
REACH (48 CSS px; canvas backing = CSS px, view.width 390 at DPR 1/2/3 — same angle, same 11.0 px plate at 150 m, same lk.px 20 at every DPR): 1× focal 1,827.6 px → 26.26 mrad · 3× → 8.75 mrad · 30× → 0.875 mrad. Plate (E, 0.495 m @150 m = 3.3 mrad) width 11 / 33 / 329 px — at 3× and 30× the r.143 under-the-pip pass locks before the reach matters. CAPITAL DOOR D-11TH @68 m: 59 / 177 / 1,774 px tall; at 1× and 3× a lock survives 2 mrad off-centre, at 30× the lock is NONE 1 mrad off while the door fills 2.7 screens — non-plate kinds have no outline pass, only a 48 px centre radius.
STICK at 30× (turret law u.pan += −rx·120°/s·zf, zf = fovDeg()/38 with view H 662 → half-angle 0.36°): full deflection 1.136°/s = 19.8 mrad/s = 0.33 mrad per 60 Hz frame; 1 px past the 8 % deadzone on the 72 px stick = 0.60 mrad/s; drag 0.0132 mrad/px. The reach is 2.6–2.7 frames of full stick at EVERY zoom (both scale 1/focal), so a thumb cannot hold the 0.875 mrad at 30× while moving — it can only stop (0 rate) and rely on the marked target's hysteresis; a 2 px tremor (1.2 mrad/s) crosses the reach in 0.7 s.
BREAKS AT: <30 fps — range MoT 1.1 unmarked at 6× throttle (29.2 rAF; the deck's own reading says 42). <15 fps (Pi floor) — range at 20×: 10.0 (1.1 free), 14.2 (5.5 free), 13.8 scaled-marked; every Capital cell at 20× sits 15.8–22.6. r.147 is not the cause: marking (lockOn → 1 µs) moves the range 20× cell from 10.0 to 19.4 only because the under-pip plateRect pass (22 plateRect + 4 platesHere filters per draw) goes with it; draw's 750–4,700 proj/frame is the budget. INSTRUMENT: loop() clamps dt to 0.05 s before acc, so state.fps ≥ 20 whenever frames exceed 50 ms — liveHal()'s `state.fps<18 → HAL.PI` is unreachable and calibrate()'s hold30 lies (24.2 shown at a true 10.0).
OWED r.148: 1) state.fps from unclamped wall-clock (fc over real elapsed) — gate: a throttled headless run where state.fps ≤ tickN/elapsed × 1.1 and HAL.PI engages under 18; 2) a projected-outline pass for every kind (door/uav/ring/foil bounds + 8 px, as r.143 gives plates) so zoom never loses a target that fills the picture — gate: `CAP_LOCK_AT_30X` (door centred, 1 mrad off → still the door); 3) cache platesHere() per frame (one filter, not 7) and skip the under-pip plateRect pass when the marked target holds — gate: calls-per-draw assertion in range-2525 (platesHere ≤ 2/draw, plateRect ≤ 12/draw on the range).
HARDWARE-GATED: every fps here is headless Chromium on a shared container under CDP throttle — a Pi 4 / a phone at DPR 3 rendering the same 390 CSS px canvas needs the reference phone and the Pi on the bench before any cell above is called a floor or a pass.

### Pangu · B

LENS: Pangu · B — THE AI MEMBER (adversarial play)
GRADE: C+ — every safety rail on the AI held (never on amber, never twice, never a qual round, never dead/off-lane, two-humans and marker's-seat in a room, hashes match), but the AI's own amber defeats the r.147 fix on the TARGET button and the AI signs half its rows as the human.
Deck r.147 sha256 2476f68a… · runs: pangu-b-{bounce,fire,qual,stay}.json (harness), pangu-b-probe.json (P1–P7), pangu-b-probe2.json (A–C), pangu-b-team-{spot,fire}.json (bent team harness, AsM on the host).

BROKE (repro):
1. TARGET BUTTON MARKS THE AI'S 50 L, NOT THE PIP'S 150 R (P1). SPOT on → AI ambers C-50L, head snaps -2.14°→5.26°. Human re-aims the pip onto the 150 R (lock before AsM = C-150R), presses #fTgt → desig = C-50L by SID (row "DESIGNATED C-50L role=HI"), head yanked back to 5.26°. Cause: lockOn() line 1934 returns state.desig.ref whenever it is in the 35° cone, before pipNearest — an ASM@ amber captures the button. Key 1 in the same state marks C-150R (slots() has no hysteresis) — so "key 1 marks what the button marks" (register r.147) is false with the AI on. The operator's "keeps resetting to 50 m left" is alive on the AsM path.
2. THE AI'S ACTIONS ARE SIGNED HI ON THE CANONICAL RECORD (probe2 A/B; team-fire rowsH/J). AsM FIRE kill: HIT row role=AI by=ASM@ME, but the SIM-ACTION row role=HI by= (decisions[] says actor=ASM@ME). Empty magazine: REJECT EMPTY_MAGAZINE row role=HI while decisions[] actor=ASM@ME. decide() → ev(kind,id,reason,extra) never passes state._shooter. The hashed, travelling events disagree with the decisions ledger on who acted; the peer's APPROVE row says "PEER HI-2" for an AI mark it approved (viaAI dropped, team-spot rowsJ).
3. THE HIT SENTENCE IS NEVER READ (probe2 C; harness bounce FIRE steps). With SPOT on and the next plate standing, the AI re-marks in the same tick: 23 ms after the human's FIRE the toast reads "AMBER · 100 M CENTRE · MARKED BY THE AI"; all three harness FIRE steps report the AMBER toast (the harness scored 0 hits / 3 "misses" while HIT went 0→3). The human sees the score strip move and never the word HIT.
4. TWO AMBER ROWS FOR ONE PLATE (P1 afterFire): tgtSlot = ["1:C-50L amber ASM","2:C-50L amber ASM"] — the AI's stale slot 1 survives the human's key-1 (slot 2) and the kill, and the AI re-marks into slot 2. The board lists the same plate twice.
5. metricsOf() COUNTS THE AI AS THE HUMAN (P1/P4/probe2): designations=5 lumps ASM@ and SID rows; no ai/hi field, no shot split. The sidecar cannot say what the AI did.
6. ON THE SHEET THE AI MARKS THE 50 M FOR EVER (P5): bounce+sheet, hit → holes=1, up=true, lifePct=100 → the AI re-marks the holed C-50 within the same tick, never the other nine (nearest-in-metres, r.147's "still open" line). The AI's mark = the target the human's stick was NOT on: the AI fires the HUMAN'S optic — stick 25° off, approve → "MISS role=AI by=ASM@ME" (P4 offAim); the AI is blamed for the human's aim.
7. After an EMPTY-MAGAZINE refusal the AI's one chance is spent (asmFired=true before fireN, P4/probe2 B): RELOAD → box stays red, AI silent, no sentence tells the human to fire it; the human pressed FIRE blind (HIT 1).

HELD:
- Never fires on amber (P4 amberHeld 400 ms; QA ASM_FIRE_NEEDS_RED); once per box (P4 offAimLater: asmFired=true, no second row 800 ms; harness fire: 3 boxes, 3 AI HITs); never a qualification round (harness qual 6/6 by HI, P6 4 rounds, asmShotRows=[]; P7 mode swap bounce→qual40 releases the amber (HOLD AUTHORITY_RELEASED) and the first qual red box is not fired by the AI, asmShot=0); never after RESET (P4: HOLD+RESET+fresh amber, no shot); never through an empty magazine (REJECT EMPTY_MAGAZINE, mag stays 0).
- Human marks first → the AI never overwrites (P2, 1500 ms) and the red box is the human's (HI-2).
- Never a dead, off-lane or lowered plate: every desig.lane===3/12 across 30+ marks; stay mode advanced 50L→…→300 with no re-mark of a fallen plate.
- Stick: the AI's aimUnitAt is one snap at mark time; a 25° drag holds 1.5 s (P3), no fight — but every kill's re-mark snaps the head again (P3 afterKillNext 30.26→5.26, P4 5.26→-5.26).
- Room (team-spot/fire, DIRECT, hashMatch=true): the AI's mark travels as NET AMBER, host self-approve HOLDs TWO_HUMANS, joiner approves, joiner FIRE refused NOT_THE_MARKERS_SEAT, host (or the host's AI in FIRE) fires once: "HIT role=AI by=ASM@host" on both records. The AI's human-marker rule (r.136) holds.

OWED r.148:
1. TARGET/LOCK with an AI amber up marks the pip's target — lockOn() takes the desig hysteresis ONLY when nothing else is within LOCK_REACH_PX of the pip (or never for an ASM@ mark); key 1 and the button agree. Gate: QA row TARGET_MARKS_THE_PIP_OVER_THE_AI_AMBER (SPOT on, AI on 50 L, pip on 150 R → button marks 150 R by SID, head moves <1°, key 1 agrees) + a range-2525 case.

The AI signs every row it causes: decide() passes state._shooter as data.by (SIM-ACTION, REJECT, HOLD role=AI); wireApprove keeps viaAI in the APPROVE row ("PEER HI OVER AI"); metricsOf() reports designationsAI/HI and shotsAI/HI. 3. The AI waits for the human to read the result and keeps one row per plate: no re-mark for ≥1.2 s after a HIT/MISS toast; designate() reuses the slot that already holds the id; after RELOAD the AI re-arms on the red box (asmFired cleared by magReload) or the toast says "RELOADED · YOU FIRE THE RED BOX". ENGLISH-ONLY: play.html has no t() and no lexicon; tests/drone-i18n.test.mjs gates only the React components (FILES L9), so the deck is English by design. If ever translated, the lexicon needs, as keyed sentences with slots (never concatenation): noLockMsg ×5 variants (L1927), the AMBER/RED BOX composites (L1919/L2289: {plate}, {seat}, {next}), the eight refusals above, bandWord ×5, plateWord's M/LEFT/RIGHT/CENTRE, strip words (TRAINING·RESET/DOWN, HIT, MISS, QUAL, READY, PHASE, ENG, UP, MAG CHANGE, NEXT SOON, NOT ENGAGED), the magazine line, LOCK, caption RED/AMBER, the QUAL phase names (L742-745 'STANDING → PRONE UNSUPPORTED' …), the eleven CIN beats and the four intro screens (L175-215).

### Thor · A

LENS: Thor · A — THE FIRE GATE
GRADE: B — every path into fireN is gated by one red-box check that precedes every scoring branch, and r.147's head nudge cannot land a shot on an unmarked plate; but commit() (the door "FIRE n" path, play.html:2386) is reachable with no red box through state.pending, and APPROVE never asks whether the approver can see the target.

THE INVARIANT: nothing is scored against a target unless that target is state.desig, its phase is 'red', and the shot goes through fireN — and fireN scores only state.desig.ref (play.html:2346 s.ref = state.desig.ref; 2374 applyHit(s.ref); 2323 plateHit(ref)/pipOff(ref) against THAT ref), so the pip can only hit the marked plate or miss it.
Members of "an input reaches fireN" (all served bytes == r.147, cmp identical; all gated at 2352 NO_RED_BOX/AMBER_NO_APPROVE, which sits before 2364 EMPTY_MAGAZINE, 2365 ring branch, 2373 pop branch):

 4 double-tap → only if pickNear(tap).id===state.desig.id → fireN :3719-3722 · 5 X/Space mark() → fireN when state.desig :2387,2390; door tag needs red :2394
 6 AI member asmTick → only phase==='red' && !asmFired && c<5 && !qual40, once, via fireN with _shooter='ASM@' :2513-2514 (probe P7: amber → 0 shots over 30 ticks; approve HI OVER AI → exactly 1 shot)
 7 spiralTest :3507,3509 · 8 boot QA/batch trials :3806,4208,4213 (simDirect, linkMute) · 9 stale slot number fireN(3) with tgtSlot[3] unset fires the red T1, not another plate (probe P6)
Extra gates inside fireN: ROUND_NOT_OPEN :2346 · NOT_THE_RED_TARGET :2348 · NOT_THE_MARKERS_SEAT :2349 · TARGET_GONE :2350 · WRONG_LANE :2353 · CH5_APPROVE_AGAIN :2354 · EMPTY_MAGAZINE :2364.
Red is made only by approveDesig :2265 (ROUND_NOT_OPEN, TARGET_DOWN :2268, TWO_HUMANS in LIVE :2276) or wireApprove :2243 (NO_MARK, NOT_AMBER, TWO_HUMANS :2251); the reducer's local APPROVE row only reds an amber that is already state.desig :2044. asm-play doubleFire: 3 refusals, second pull "NO RED BOX" (thorA-asm.log).

### Thor · B

LENS: Thor · B — THE FIRE GATE (attack)
GRADE: C — every local input is refused on amber with a row and the r.147 shot lands on the approved plate, but over the wire one phone can forge the host's APPROVE by a claimed `by` field and a peer HIT row downs an unmarked plate on the host with no red box and no row.

THE INVARIANT: nothing fires unless `approveDesig`/`wireApprove` turned THIS box red under the two-humans rule, every refusal is a decision row, and a peer's row is only as true as the gate that wrote it.

BROKE (repro):
1. **Forged approver over the wire** — play.html:1518 `const by=m.by||pay.by||m.peerId||m.sid` feeds `wireApprove(id,by)`; the two-humans test is `by!==d.by && members[by]`. Repro (thor-b-room.mjs R5): J `targetN(1)` → J `linkSend(Object.assign(commEnv('APPROVE',{id,by:state.com.hostId}),{id,by:state.com.hostId}))` → host `state.desig.phase==='red'`, host `state.decisions` gains `APPROVE approvedBy=<host SID>`. One phone marked, "approved as the host", and (R5b) fired; the host's record shows a decision the host never took. The r.135 fix for DESIG `data.by` was not applied to APPROVE.
2. **A peer HIT row needs no red box** — applyWorld (play.html:1998-2009) downs a plate and tallies +1 on any peer HIT row. Repro (R6, and solo A8 via `applyWorld` directly): J sends `HIT` for `C-100L-L21` with an embedded row `peerId=SID` → host plate `up:false life:0`, `rangeHit+1`, "THE OTHER SEAT HIT", no DESIGNATED/APPROVE on the record. The 1418 guard trips only on the sender's echo.
3. **Blind approve** — `approveDesig` (2265) never asks whether the target is on the approver's picture. Repro (R4): C-300 at x=403 of 390 px on the host, host taps APPROVE, accepted, J fires, HIT.
HELD: every local input on amber (8 inputs), every consumer except MAP releases the red with a row, hold = one shot, AI fires once and only on red, dead/off-lane forged reds refused, the r.147 shot lands on the approved plate and never on the plate under the pip (A14, B4), marker≠approver≠shooter in a room (R1, R2, R7).

OWED r.148:
1. APPROVE takes its author from the authenticated envelope (`m.peerId`), never `m.by`/`pay.by` (1518) — gate: QA row `WIRE_APPROVE_AUTHOR_FROM_ENVELOPE` + a two-phone e2e step (drone-team-e2e.mjs) that sends a `by=host` APPROVE from the joiner and asserts HOLD TWO_HUMANS.
2. A peer HIT/MISS row is applied only when this record holds an APPROVE for that id (or it is this device's red box); otherwise `REJECT HIT_WITHOUT_RED_BOX` row — gate: QA row `PEER_HIT_NEEDS_RED_ON_RECORD` + e2e forged-HIT step.
3. The picture is part of the gate: `approveDesig` HOLDs `TARGET_NOT_ON_PICTURE` when the target projects off the approver's picture; `fireN` REJECTs `TARGET_OFF_PICTURE` instead of spending a round (B1); MAP releases the box or hides FIRE (B5); a pull on a held red writes a row and training caps rounds per approval (B2) — gate: QA rows in drone-deck-qa (test:ci).

HARDWARE-GATED: real trigger/laser and its debounce (hold-to-fire measured on a mouse only); SpeechRecognition (headless Chromium has none — shimmed, so the phrase gate is proven, the recognizer is not); real-phone double-tap timing (320 ms window measured with page.mouse); WebRTC over a real network (ICE here on loopback); a peer under a compromised phone (R5b) cannot be gated on the shooter's device at all — only by the record on the other seat (owed 1–2).
